local function fail() return 20 end
local function mul32(a, b)
  local M = 4294967296
  a, b = a % M, b % M
  local a_lo, a_hi = a % 65536, math.floor(a / 65536)
  local b_lo, b_hi = b % 65536, math.floor(b / 65536)
  return ((a_lo * b_lo) + (((a_lo * b_hi + a_hi * b_lo) % 65536) * 65536)) % M
end
local function mkbit()
  local src = bit32 or bit
  local M = 4294967296
  local function bxor(a, b)
    if src and src.bxor then return src.bxor(a, b) end
    local r, p = 0, 1
    a, b = a % M, b % M
    for _ = 1, 32 do
      if (a % 2) ~= (b % 2) then r = r + p end
      a, b = math.floor(a / 2), math.floor(b / 2)
      p = p * 2
    end
    return r
  end
  local function lshift(a, n)
    if src and src.lshift then return src.lshift(a, n) end
    return (math.floor(a % M) * (2 ^ (n % 32))) % M
  end
  local function rshift(a, n)
    if src and src.rshift then return src.rshift(a, n) end
    return math.floor((a % M) / (2 ^ (n % 32)))
  end
  return { bxor = bxor, lshift = lshift, rshift = rshift }
end
local B = mkbit()
local function fnv(s)
  local h = 2166136261
  for i = 1, #s do
    h = B.bxor(h, string.byte(s, i))
    h = mul32(h, 16777619)
  end
  return h
end
if fnv(MARK .. "|" .. PASS) ~= EXPECT then return fail() end
local CONST = B.bxor(0x197363, EXPECT)
local SEED = mul32(CONST, 0x9E3779B9)
local ALPH = "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz!#$%&()*+,-./:;<=>?@~"
local function b83dec(s)
  if #s % 8 ~= 0 then return nil end
  local map = {}
  for i = 1, #ALPH do map[ALPH:sub(i, i)] = i - 1 end
  local parts = {}
  for g = 1, #s, 8 do
    local n = 0
    for i = 0, 7 do
      local v = map[s:sub(g + i, g + i)]
      if not v then return nil end
      n = n * 83 + v
    end
    local blk = {}
    for i = 6, 1, -1 do
      blk[i] = string.char(n % 256)
      n = math.floor(n / 256)
    end
    parts[#parts + 1] = table.concat(blk)
  end
  return table.concat(parts)
end
local function xs(s)
  s = s % 4294967296
  s = B.bxor(s, B.lshift(s, 13) % 4294967296)
  s = B.bxor(s, B.rshift(s, 17))
  s = B.bxor(s, B.lshift(s, 5) % 4294967296)
  return s % 4294967296
end
local function keystream(n, pc)
  local s = B.bxor(B.bxor(SEED, pc), CONST)
  local t = {}
  for i = 0, n - 1 do
    s = xs((s + CONST + i) % 4294967296)
    t[i + 1] = B.rshift(s, 16) % 256
  end
  return t
end
local pieces = {}
for ci = 1, #C do
  local cell = C[ci]
  if cell:sub(1, 4) ~= "crs%" then return fail() end
  local raw = b83dec(cell:sub(5))
  if not raw then return fail() end
  local ln = string.byte(raw, 1)
  local mac = string.byte(raw, 2) + string.byte(raw, 3) * 256 + string.byte(raw, 4) * 65536 + string.byte(raw, 5) * 16777216
  local body = raw:sub(6, 5 + ln)
  local kb = keystream(#body, (ci - 1) * 0x100)
  local t = {}
  for i = 1, #body do
    local by = B.bxor(string.byte(body, i), kb[i])
    by = B.bxor(by, (i * 37 + CONST) % 256)
    t[i] = string.char(by)
  end
  local pt = table.concat(t)
  local m2 = 0
  for i = 1, #pt do m2 = (m2 * 131 + string.byte(pt, i)) % 4294967296 end
  if m2 ~= mac then return fail() end
  pieces[ci] = pt
  C[ci] = ""
end
local decoded = table.concat(pieces)
local ls = loadstring or load
if not ls then return fail() end
local fn = ls(decoded)
decoded = ""
if not fn then return fail() end
return fn()
